```
1	# Runbook & FAQ
2	
3	## Release checklist (v1)
4	
5	- All CI gates green on `main`; no open release-blocking issues on the current milestone.
6	- Backend image built + pushed to GHCR with an **immutable** tag (not `latest`).
7	- DB migrations are backward-compatible; the migrate job runs `alembic upgrade head`
8	  **before** the app rolls.
9	- Deploy via the **Deploy** workflow (`workflow_dispatch`); verify `/healthz` 200,
10	  `/api/v1/me` 401 (auth enforced), SPA + deep links load.
11	- Docs site published (this site) and linked from the README.
12	
13	Full deploy steps + verification: **[deploy/README.md](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/blob/main/deploy/README.md)**.
14	
15	## Live smoke (deployed stack + harness data)
16	
17	Automated, opt-in (never CI):
18	
19	1. **API-level:** `DATAQ_API=https://<frontend-host> DATAQ_BEARER=<AAD token> python -m
20	   backend.scripts.e2e_smoke` — read + authoring round-trips against the live API
21	   (12 checks).
22	2. **Browser-level:** `E2E_LIVE_BASE_URL=https://<frontend-host> pnpm e2e` in
23	   `frontend/` — a headed one-time OIDC sign-in, then read-only specs (dashboard KPIs,
24	   live suite + checks, run-detail). See
25	   [frontend/e2e/README.md](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/blob/main/frontend/e2e/README.md).
26	
27	Manual checklist (the mutating tail):
28	
29	- Trigger a live suite run (Run now) on a harness suite → completes green on real
30	  warehouse/file data.
31	- Let a harness pipeline (ADF or Airflow) succeed → the bound suite auto-runs and
32	  correlates on **Results → Pipelines**.
33	- Force a failing run → the Teams/Slack/email alert arrives with the right severity;
34	  a repeat failure is deduped.
35	- MCP: point Claude Desktop at `https://<frontend-host>/mcp/` (trailing slash — see
36	  [AI assistants (MCP setup)](mcp-setup.md)) and run the 4 canonical queries
37	  (what failed today / run suite X / why did pipeline Y fail / add a null check).
38
```

> AGENT

All five are done, validated, and green locally. On your cadence question: **agreed and encoded** — the CHANGELOG header and the release checklist now both state the policy: one curated entry per minor/major release written at tag time, patch releases only when user-visible or security-relevant, raw material regenerated from `git log v<prev>..HEAD` (your squash-merged conventional commits make this cheap), mirrored into the GitHub Release body. Per-PR changelog lines are explicitly not the convention.

What landed (all in the working tree, uncommitted, alongside the earlier ER diagram):

**[architecture.md](docs/architecture.md)** — four new sections, every diagram validated through the Mermaid renderer and grounded in the actual code, not the ADR prose:
- **Runtime flows** — two sequence diagrams: the suite run lifecycle (trigger → queued run → broker → worker → SecretStore → GX/monitor execution → severity banding → results → best-effort alert publish, with the broker-down, cooperative-cancel, and batch-absent-→-skip edge cases as notes) and the orchestration event flow (webhook auth per provider, AlertPing poll-now branch, `pipeline_runs` upsert, trigger-binding match with the #308 dedup index, failure-never-triggers rule, and the 10-min poll + gap-recovery fallback). Each ends with links to the source modules.
- **Status semantics** — a state diagram for `runs.status` (including both cancel paths and the reaper) and a derivation flowchart for `results.status` making the two things easiest to get wrong un-missable: run status is execution not data quality, and `skip`/`error` are excluded from the health-score denominator.
- **Trust boundaries & authentication** — the one-diagram consolidation of ADRs 0006/0007/0008/0028: what crosses each boundary and with what credential, plus notes on defense-in-depth JWT validation, the only non-JWT endpoints, and fail-closed MCP.

**[test_architecture_er_sync.py](backend/tests/db/test_architecture_er_sync.py)** — the drift guard: every `Base.metadata` table must appear in the ER diagram and vice versa, with a self-sanity test so the guard can't pass vacuously. I confirmed the regex extracts exactly the 13 real tables. 3/3 pass; ruff/black/mypy clean; Bandit's B101 hits don't apply because the CI gate runs `-c pyproject.toml`, which excludes `backend/tests`.

**[CHANGELOG.md](CHANGELOG.md)** — Keep-a-Changelog format with the cadence policy in the header, an `[Unreleased]` section, and a curated `[1.0.0] — 2026-07-04` backfill covering the deployer/user-visible surface. **[runbook-faq.md](docs/runbook-faq.md)** got the matching release-checklist step.

One fix along the way: `mkdocs build --strict` rejects relative links pointing outside `docs/`, so all backend source links (including the one in the earlier ER section) now use absolute GitHub URLs, matching the runbook's existing convention. Strict build is clean.

Deferred per your earlier framing: the deeper threat-model doc stays a pre-marketplace item — the trust-boundary section is its seed.

Ready to branch (`docs/architecture-behavior-diagrams`), commit in logical units (`docs:` diagrams, `test:` drift guard, `docs:` changelog+policy), and open the PR with the milestone — just say go.

```json
  "old_string": "```mermaid\nstateDiagram-v2\n    [*] --> queued : run created (manual · schedule · trigger binding)\n    queued --> running : worker picks up the task\n    queued --> cancelled : user cancel — task revoked + cooperative start-check\n    queued --> failed : dispatch / setup / target failure — or reaper past threshold (#309)\n    running --> succeeded : execution completed (even when checks FAILED — run status is execution, not data quality)\n    running --> failed : adapter raised — or worker died and the reaper swept it (#309)\n    running --> cancelled : user cancel (DB status set; worker honours cooperatively)\n    succeeded --> [*]\n    failed --> [*]\n    cancelled --> [*]\n```",
  "new_string": "```mermaid\nstateDiagram-v2\n    [*] --> queued : created (manual · schedule · trigger)\n    queued --> running : worker picks up\n    queued --> cancelled : user cancel\n    queued --> failed : dispatch/setup failure · reaper (#309)\n    running --> succeeded : execution completed\n    running --> cancelled : user cancel\n    running --> failed : adapter raised · reaper (#309)\n    succeeded --> [*]\n    failed --> [*]\n    cancelled --> [*]\n```\n\n- **`succeeded` means executed** — checks may still have failed; the data-quality outcome lives in `results.status`.\n- **Cancel works on any non-terminal run:** the API sets `cancelled` and best-effort revokes the Celery task; the worker also honours the status cooperatively (start-check before executing).\n- **The reaper (#309)** drives runs orphaned in `queued`/`running` past a threshold (task never published, or the worker died mid-run) to terminal `failed`."
}
```

```
sequenceDiagram
    autonumber
    participant ORC as ADF / Airflow
    participant FE as Frontend nginx (public ingress)
    participant API as FastAPI (internal)
    participant KV as SecretStore (Key Vault)
    participant PG as PostgreSQL
    participant BR as Redis broker
    participant W as Celery worker

    Note over ORC: pipeline / DAG run reaches a terminal state
    ORC->>FE: POST /api/v1/orchestration/events/{provider}<br/>(ADF — Azure Monitor alert · Airflow — on_*_callback snippet)
    FE->>API: same-origin proxy
    API->>KV: load receiver secret
    API->>API: authenticate — ADF constant-time token query param (ADR 0006)<br/>· Airflow HMAC-SHA256 over raw body (ADR 0007)
    API->>API: OrchestrationProvider.parse → RunUpdate or AlertPing

    alt AlertPing — run-anonymous Azure Monitor alert (#35;492)
        API->>BR: enqueue targeted poll-now (provider + resource)
        API-->>ORC: 202 reconciling
    else RunUpdate
        API->>PG: upsert pipeline_runs ON (provider, provider_run_id)
        alt status = succeeded
            API->>PG: match enabled trigger_bindings (provider, pipeline_or_dag_id, env)
            API->>PG: INSERT runs, triggered_by = provider:pipeline:run_id<br/>ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING (dedup index, #308)
            API->>BR: dispatch run_suite per triggered run
        else status = failed
            API->>API: alert the user only — failures never trigger suites
        end
        API-->>ORC: 200 recorded
    end

    Note over W,PG: polling fallback (#35;171) — a 10-min beat sweeps every orchestrator connection<br/>via the provider REST API (list_recent_runs, 15-min lookback) into the SAME<br/>upsert + trigger path — a 30-min gap-recovery sweep (1-hour window) covers downtime
```

> AGENT

Good catch — it wasn't just cosmetic. Everything is fixed and verified now.

**What was odd:** the semicolon in the label `user cancel (DB status set; worker honours cooperatively)` terminated the Mermaid statement mid-line, so the leftovers rendered as four floating orphan boxes (`;`, `worker`, `honours`, `cooperatively)`) across the top of the state diagram. The other labels were also long enough to wrap into grey slabs bigger than the states themselves. The syntax validator happily passed all of it — the failure was only visible in the render.

**The fix:** the state diagram now has short edge labels (`user cancel`, `adapter raised · reaper (#309)` …) and the nuance moved into three prose bullets under it (succeeded ≠ checks passed, cancel semantics, reaper). I extracted the actual PNG render this time — it's compact and clean.

**Chasing that led to a second, worse bug you hadn't hit yet:** in *sequence* diagrams, Mermaid treats `#…;` as HTML-entity escape syntax, and every `#NNN` issue reference was silently eating its line — the run-lifecycle diagram rendered `never left stuck queued (` with `#227)` gone, and the orchestration diagram's entire polling-fallback note truncated to `polling fallback (`. GitHub uses the same engine, so both would have shipped mangled. Flowcharts and state diagrams are unaffected (`(#122)`/`(#309)` render fine there). Fix: the six refs inside the two sequence diagrams now use Mermaid's documented escape `#35;227` etc., which renders as `(#227)` — I verified each one comes through intact in the rendered SVG, along with the previously-swallowed gap-recovery text.

`mkdocs build --strict` is still green and no bare `#NNN` refs remain in any sequence block. The practical lesson baked into this session: for Mermaid, *valid* ≠ *renders correctly* — sequence-diagram text with `#` or `;` needs a render check, not just a syntax check. Want me to add that one-liner as a caution note next to the sync rule at the top of architecture.md?

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/arijit/Coding/Python/DataQ/docs/architecture.md",
  "old_string": "> Keep these diagrams in sync with the code. When a new component, datasource, integration, or DB table is added, update the relevant diagram in the same PR.",
  "new_string": "> Keep these diagrams in sync with the code. When a new component, datasource, integration, or DB table is added, update the relevant diagram in the same PR.\n>\n> ⚠️ **Mermaid gotcha — syntactically *valid* ≠ *renders correctly*.** In **sequence-diagram** text, `#` starts Mermaid's HTML-entity escape (`#35;` → `#`) and a stray `;` terminates a statement — a bare `#NNN` issue ref or an inline semicolon silently truncates the rendered line while the syntax check still passes. Write issue refs in sequence diagrams as `#35;NNN` (renders as `#NNN`; flowcharts/state/ER diagrams don't need this) and eyeball the rendered diagram before merging, not just the linter."
}
```

```json
  "command": "gh pr create --base main --milestone \"Backlog (post-v1 / testing)\" --title \"docs: architecture ER/sequence/state/trust diagrams + ER drift guard + CHANGELOG backfill\" --body \"$(cat <<'EOF'\n## What\n\nCloses the behavior-documentation gap in `docs/architecture.md` (which showed structure but never ordering/time), plus the two supporting artifacts:\n\n- **Data model (ER diagram)** — all 13 tables from `backend/app/db/models.py` with PK/FK/UK markers, constrained value sets, delete behavior per edge, and the two **no-FK soft links** (`pipeline_runs ⇢ runs` via the `triggered_by` marker; `trigger_bindings ⇢ pipeline_runs` via the `(provider, pipeline, env)` match) drawn dotted. Reading notes cover the ADR 0020 cascade posture, singleton constraints, and secrets-are-refs.\n- **Runtime flows (sequence diagrams)** — suite run lifecycle (trigger → dispatch → GX/monitor execution → severity banding → results → best-effort alert publish, with broker-down/cancel/batch-absent edges) and orchestration event flow (per-provider webhook auth, AlertPing poll-now, trigger-binding dedup, 10-min poll + gap-recovery fallback).\n- **Status semantics** — `runs.status` state diagram + `results.status` derivation flowchart, making the two load-bearing rules un-missable: run status is *execution not data quality*, and `skip`/`error` are excluded from the health-score denominator.\n- **Trust boundaries & authentication** — one-diagram consolidation of ADRs 0006/0007/0008/0028 with boundary notes (defense-in-depth JWT validation, the only non-JWT endpoints, fail-closed MCP).\n- **ER drift guard** (`backend/tests/db/test_architecture_er_sync.py`) — table-level, both directions: schema tables must appear in the diagram and diagram entities must exist in `Base.metadata`.\n- **CHANGELOG.md** — Keep-a-Changelog backfill for v1.0.0 + the cadence policy (curated entry per minor/major at tag time, not per PR), mirrored into the runbook release checklist.\n\n## Notes\n\n- Every diagram was validated **and render-checked** (not just syntax): the Mermaid validator passes sequence-diagram text that renders truncated — `#NNN` issue refs and inline `;` are entity/statement syntax. Refs in sequence diagrams use the `#35;NNN` escape; a gotcha note now sits next to the sync rule at the top of the doc.\n- Backend source links use absolute GitHub URLs — `mkdocs build --strict` rejects relative links outside `docs/` (matches the runbook's existing convention). Strict build green.\n- Local battery: ruff / black / mypy / bandit clean; full backend suite 1291 passed, 98.29% coverage.\n\n🤖 Generated with [Claude Code](https://claude.com/claude-code)\nEOF\n)\"",
  "description": "Open PR with milestone"
}
```

```
## Summary of PR #586

**Intent:** Close the behavior-documentation gap in `docs/architecture.md` by adding diagrams showing runtime ordering, data model, status semantics, and authentication trust boundaries. Includes a schema drift guard and CHANGELOG backfill.

**Files touched (487 additions, 1 deletion):**

1. **CHANGELOG.md** (new file) — Keep-a-Changelog format with v1.0.0 entry. Documents datasources, checks, execution, orchestration, results, alerting, access, AI/MCP, and deployment. Establishes cadence: one curated entry per minor/major release at tag time, not per PR; patch releases only if user-visible or security-relevant.

2. **backend/tests/db/test_architecture_er_sync.py** (new file) — Drift guard test ensuring the ER diagram in `docs/architecture.md` stays in sync with the actual schema. Bidirectional validation: every table in `Base.metadata` must appear in the diagram, and every diagram entity must correspond to a real table.

3. **docs/architecture.md** (substantially expanded) — Five new major sections:
   - **ER diagram:** all 13 tables with PK/FK/UK markers, constrained value sets, delete cascade behavior, and two soft-link dotted lines (`pipeline_runs ⇢ runs` via `triggered_by` marker; `trigger_bindings ⇢ pipeline_runs` via composite key match).
   - **Suite run lifecycle:** sequence diagram from trigger (manual/scheduled/orchestration) through dispatch, GX/monitor execution, severity banding, results, and best-effort alert publish.
   - **Orchestration event flow:** per-provider webhook auth (ADF secret-in-URL / Airflow HMAC), AlertPing poll-now, trigger-binding dedup, and 10-min polling fallback with gap recovery.
   - **Status semantics:** run state diagram + result status derivation flowchart; documents the two load-bearing rules: run status = *execution* not DQ quality, and `skip`/`error` excluded from health-score denominator.
   - **Trust boundaries & authentication:** consolidated ADRs 0006/0007/0008/0028 showing credential flows, defense-in-depth JWT validation, webhook secret schemes, and fail-closed MCP.

4. **docs/runbook-faq.md** — Added CHANGELOG checklist step to the release checklist.

**Notes:** All diagrams validated and render-checked (not just syntax-valid). Backend source links use absolute GitHub URLs. Local test battery clean: ruff/black/mypy/bandit passed; 1291 tests at 98.29% coverage.
```

```json
{
```

```json
  "old_string": "- **Conventions (elided from the diagram for noise):** every table has a `gen_random_uuid()` UUID PK and `created_at`; mutable entities also carry `updated_at`. Status/type columns are `TEXT` + `CHECK` constraints, **not** native PG enums (migration ergonomics).",
  "new_string": "- **Conventions (elided from the diagram for noise):** every table has a `gen_random_uuid()` UUID PK and `created_at`; user-editable entities also carry `updated_at` (`runs`/`results` deliberately don't — they are engine-mutated event rows whose lifecycle lives in `started_at`/`finished_at`/`sample_failures_purged_at`). Status/type columns are `TEXT` + `CHECK` constraints, **not** native PG enums (migration ergonomics).\n- **`shares.permission` grants are `view`/`edit` only.** `admin` is legal in the DB `CHECK` but never granted (`share_service.GRANTABLE_PERMISSIONS`): workspace-admin is *implicit* on every suite (config allowlist, not a share row) and `owner` is `suites.created_by`, not a share — ADR [0027](adr/0027-suite-permission-model-workspace-admin.md)."
}
```

> AGENT

**PR [#586](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/pull/586) is open, reviewed, fixed, and fully green — all 14 CI checks pass.** It's ready for you to squash-merge.

Full trail for this round:

**Note added** — the Mermaid gotcha now sits in the header blockquote of [architecture.md](docs/architecture.md): syntactically *valid* ≠ *renders correctly*; in sequence-diagram text `#` starts an entity escape and `;` terminates a statement, so issue refs there must be written `#35;NNN` and diagrams should be render-checked, not just linted.

**PR opened** — three commits on `docs/architecture-diagrams-and-changelog`, milestone "Backlog (post-v1 / testing)": the diagram set, the ER drift-guard test, and the CHANGELOG + runbook cadence step. Local battery ran first: ruff/black/mypy/bandit clean, full suite 1291 passed at 98.29% coverage, `mkdocs build --strict` green.

**Multi-agent review ran** (per the standing review-before-merge agreement) — five parallel reviewers, six candidate findings, each independently confidence-scored. One cleared the ≥80 posting bar and is on the PR as [the review comment](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/pull/586#issuecomment-4883249098): the orchestration diagram claimed a `202 reconciling` ack, but the webhook receivers always return HTTP 200 — `reconciling` is a response-body field.

**All six findings fixed** in fixup commit `e10b6dd5` rather than deferring the five sub-threshold ones (they were real, just lower-stakes): the 202→200 ack, the alert publish order (it's suppression → dedup → publish, with severity routing *inside* the publisher), a `shares.permission` note so `admin` doesn't read as grantable (ADR 0027), the `updated_at` convention claim corrected (`runs`/`results` deliberately don't have one), the trust-diagram token edge redrawn so AI clients don't appear to drive an IDP flow (ADR 0008), and `pipeline_runs.last_updated_at` added to the ER diagram (it's load-bearing in the polling staleness check). Every edited diagram was re-validated *and* render-verified, and the drift test + strict docs build re-ran green before the push.

```
# DataQ — Progress tracker (post-v1)

> The **live task tracker**, active since `v1.0.0` (tagged 2026-07-04). The completed v1
> ledger — the per-PR record of the 8-week roadmap, Weeks 1–8 — is **archived, frozen at the
> tag, in [progress-v1.md](progress-v1.md)** (companion: [retro-v1.md](retro-v1.md)).
> **Updated at the end of every PR** — the PR template has a checkbox to enforce.
> Source of truth for "what's done vs. what's left" in the current cycle. CLAUDE.md §13
> carries only the headline.

## Status legend

| Symbol | Meaning |
|---|---|
| ✅ | Done — PR merged to `main` |
| 🟡 | In progress — open PR or partially shipped |
| ⬜ | Not started |
| 🔵 | Deferred / scope-changed (with note) |

---

## Snapshot

| | |
|---|---|
| **v1 baseline** | `v1.0.0` tagged 2026-07-04 — 187/189 roadmap tasks (~99%); all 8 weekly exit gates met; deployed to Azure Container Apps; retro at [retro-v1.md](retro-v1.md); full ledger at [progress-v1.md](progress-v1.md) |
| **Current cycle** | **Post-v1 cycle planning — not started.** Input: [context/post-v1-roadmap.md](../context/post-v1-roadmap.md) (55 issues across 14 themes + the gap register G-a…G-i). Recommended opening sequence per that doc: Theme-1 `schema_drift` + `anomaly` monitor kinds (rides the ADR 0012 seam) → scale-aware execution (gap G-b, Theme 7) → incident/lineage design doc (G-d). |
| **Open issues** | **55** (verified against GitHub 2026-07-04), all on the `Backlog (post-v1 / testing)` milestone — mapped by theme in [post-v1-roadmap.md](../context/post-v1-roadmap.md), not duplicated here (#583/#584 filed 2026-07-04 from the retired `WEEK8_TODO` carry-over) |
| **Open PRs** | none |
| **Coverage gates (CI-enforced, ≥80%)** | backend `--cov-fail-under=80` (98.4% / 1,289 tests at the tag) · frontend all-src `lines: 80` (~88% / 337 tests at the tag) — every post-v1 PR rides the same gates |

---

the source of truth for issue state; this register mirrors it so nothing carried is lost.
```

```
## How to update this file

When merging a PR:

1. Find the task(s) it implements in the relevant cycle section (once the cycle plan exists).
2. Flip `⬜` → `✅` (or `⬜` → `🟡` if partial).
3. Append the PR link: `— [PR #N](https://github.com/.../pull/N)`.
4. Update the cycle subtotal and the **Snapshot** table (open PRs/issues).
5. If the PR closes a carried-over item above, strike it through with the closing ref.
6. If the PR added out-of-scope work, add a row with a note (same honesty rule as v1).

PR-template checkbox enforces this. If the change is purely tooling / docs that doesn't map
to a tracked task, tick the "N/A" checkbox.
```
